## Pacific Fishery Regulations, 1993 {#pacific-fishery-regulations-1993 .Title-of-Act}

[SOR]{.abbr title="Statutory Orders and Regulations"}/93-54
:::

[FISHERIES ACT](/eng/acts/F-14)

Registration 1993-02-04

Pacific Fishery Regulations, 1993

P.C. 1993-187 1993-02-04
::::

:::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: {.section .order}
His Excellency the Governor General in Council, on the recommendation of
the Minister of Fisheries and Oceans, pursuant to sections 8,
43[[Footnote ]{.wb-invisible}^\*^](#fn_1e){#fn_1e-ID0E2FAA}, 46 and
subsection 87(2) of the [Fisheries Act](/eng/acts/F-14), is pleased
hereby to revoke the British Columbia Fishery (General) Regulations,
made by Order in Council P.C. 1984-966 of March 22, 1984[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^\*\*^](#fn_2e){#fn_2e-ID0EVFAA}, the Pacific Coast
Marine Plant Regulations, C.R.C., c. 822, the Pacific Commercial Salmon
Fishery Regulations, C.R.C., c. 823, the Pacific Fishery Regulations,
1984, made by Order in Council P.C. 1984-1405 of April 20,
1984[[Footnote ]{.wb-invisible}^\*\*\*^](#fn_3e){#fn_3e-ID0ENFAA}, the
Pacific Herring Fishery Regulations, made by Order in Council P.C.
1984-1320 of April 18, 1984[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^\*\*\*\*^](#fn_4e){#fn_4e-ID0EJFAA}, the Pacific
Shellfish Regulations, C.R.C., c. 826, and the Tuna Fishery Regulations,
made by Order in Council P.C. 1984-2268 of June 28, 1984[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^\*\*\*\*\*^](#fn_5e){#fn_5e-ID0EDFAA}, and to make the
annexed Regulations respecting fishing in the Pacific Ocean and the
Province of British Columbia, 1993, in substitution therefor, effective
February 24, 1993.

- ::: Footnote

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-956181 .Part}

- **[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In these
  Regulations,

  [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Fisheries
      Act](/eng/acts/F-14); ([Loi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in section 1
      of the [Pacific Fishery Management Area Regulations,
      2007](/eng/regulations/SOR-2007-77); ([secteur]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[bait herring]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[bait herring]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2022-196, s.
      13\]]{.Repealed}

  [[band]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[band]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/93-334, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[barbless hook]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[barbless hook]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/99-296, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[beam trawl net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[beam trawl net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a trawl net the mouth
      of which is held open by a beam or pole that is held horizontally
      across the mouth of the net while the net is under tow; ([chalut à
      perche]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[bottom trawl]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[bottom trawl]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a trawl net that is
      towed along the bed of the water; ([chalut de
      fond]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[corkline to web distance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[corkline to web distance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      shortest distance between the corkline of a gill net and the
      webbing of the gill net; ([distance entre la ralingue et la
      nappe]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Department]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Department]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Department of
      Fisheries and Oceans; ([ministère]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[dip net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[dip net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a bag-like net, fixed to a
      frame to which a handle is attached, that is used to catch fish
      without enmeshing them; ([épuisette]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[drag seine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[drag seine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a net, weighted at the
      bottom and floated at the top, that is used to enclose an area of
      water and is then drawn ashore; ([senne
      traînante]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[fish landing station]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[fish landing station]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2022-196, s. 13\]]{.Repealed}

  [[fishwheel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[fishwheel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a frame with a wheel to
      which are attached devices to scoop fish from the water; ([filet
      rotatif]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[food herring]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[food herring]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means herring taken for human
      consumption but does not include roe herring or herring spawn on
      kelp; ([hareng de consommation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[gill net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[gill net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a net that is used to catch
      fish by enmeshing them and that does not enclose an area of
      water; ([filet maillant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[herring enclosure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[herring enclosure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any type of
      enclosure used or intended to be used to confine live herring or
      herring spawn on kelp; ([parc à harengs]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[hook and line]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[hook and line]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes longline and
      trolling gear; ([hameçon et ligne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Indian food fishing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Indian food fishing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/93-334, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Inside Salmon Troll Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Inside Salmon Troll Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/96-330, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[licensed facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[licensed facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a facility used by a person who holds a licence
        issued under the British Columbia Fish and Seafood Act, SBC
        2015, c. 14, as amended from time to time, for the purpose of
        carrying out activities authorized by the licence, whether or
        not the facility is specifically referenced in the licence; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a vessel in respect of which a licence has been
        issued under subsection 18(4); ([installation
        autorisée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[longline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[longline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a line that has a hook or
      hooks attached to it and that is anchored to the bed of the
      water; ([palangre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[mesh size]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[mesh size]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the total length of twine
      measured along two contiguous sides of a single mesh, including
      the distance across the knot joining those sides but not including
      any other knots; ([maillage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[midwater trawl]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[midwater trawl]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a trawl net other
      than a bottom trawl; ([chalut pélagique]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[non-tidal waters]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[non-tidal waters]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the waters of the
      Province other than tidal waters; ([eaux sans
      marée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[otter trawl net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[otter trawl net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a trawl net the
      mouth of which is held open by boards of wood or metal or both,
      referred to as otter boards or doors, while the net is under
      tow; ([chalut à panneaux]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Outside Salmon Troll Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Outside Salmon Troll Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/96-330, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[owner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[owner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a vessel, means the
      person or [[aboriginal organization]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as
      defined in section 2 of the Aboriginal Communal Fishing Licences
      Regulations, in whose name the vessel is
      registered; ([propriétaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Province]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Province]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Province of British
      Columbia; ([province]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[purse seine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[purse seine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a net that is drawn
      around a school of fish and then closed at the bottom by means of
      a line passing through rings attached along the lower edge of the
      net; ([senne coulissante]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Regional Director-General]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Regional Director-General]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      Regional Director-General of the Department for the Pacific
      Region; ([directeur général régional]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[registered]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[registered]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means registered with the
      Department pursuant to section 19; ([enregistré]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[ring net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ring net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a bag-shaped net that is
      hung on a frame to which a line is
      attached; ([bolinche]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[roe herring]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[roe herring]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} herring found in or taken from the waters of
        Subareas 6-1, 6-2, 8-13, 8-14 and 8-15 during the period
        beginning on May 1 and ending on June 30, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} herring found in or taken from any waters not
        referred to in paragraph (a) during the period beginning on
        February 10 and ending on April 30; ([hareng prêt à
        frayer]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[salmon]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[salmon]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes steelhead
      trout; ([saumon]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Salmon Area F]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Salmon Area F]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the waters of Areas 1
      to 11, 101 to 111, 130 and 142; ([secteur F du
      saumon]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Salmon Area G]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Salmon Area G]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the waters of Subareas
      12-5 to 12-16, Areas 20 to 27 and 121 to 127; ([secteur G du
      saumon]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Salmon Area H]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Salmon Area H]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the waters of Areas 12
      to 19, 28 and 29; ([secteur H du saumon]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[seine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[seine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a purse seine and a drag
      seine; ([senne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[shellfish]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[shellfish]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means [Mollusca]{.otherLang
      lang="la"}, [Echinodermata]{.otherLang lang="la"} and
      [Crustacea]{.otherLang lang="la"}; ([mollusques]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"}*et*[crustacés]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Subarea]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Subarea]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in section
      1 of the [Pacific Fishery Management Area Regulations,
      2007](/eng/regulations/SOR-2007-77); ([sous-secteur]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[surfline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[surfline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      section 1 of the [Pacific Fishery Management Area Regulations,
      2007](/eng/regulations/SOR-2007-77); ([ligne de
      démarcation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[tidal waters]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[tidal waters]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the waters of the Areas
      set out in Schedule 2 to the [Pacific Fishery Management Area
      Regulations, 2007](/eng/regulations/SOR-2007-77); ([eaux à
      marée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[trap]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[trap]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an enclosure that is designed
      to catch fish but does not include a trawl net or a purse
      seine; ([casier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[trap net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[trap net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/99-296, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[trawl net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[trawl net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any bag-type net that is
      dragged in the water by a vessel for the purpose of catching
      fish; ([chalut]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[trip]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[trip]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/95-181, s. 1;
      SOR/99-296, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[trolling]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[trolling]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means fishing with a hook or
      hooks attached to a line that is towed through the water by or
      from a vessel. ([pêche à la traîne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[validation tab]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[validation tab]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2013-37, s.
      5\]]{.Repealed}

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A reference in these Regulations to a species or
  group of species of fish by its common name as set out in column I of
  an item of Schedule I shall be construed as a reference to the species
  or group of species of fish whose scientific name is set out in column
  II of that item.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} All references in these Regulations to measurements
  of mesh size, depth, length or any other dimension of a net shall be
  construed as references to measurements taken when the net is wet.

## [Application]{.HTitleText1} {#h-956237 .Part}

- **[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to subsection (2), these Regulations apply in respect of the
  management and control of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} fisheries in Canadian fisheries waters in the
    Pacific Ocean and the Province;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} fishing for tuna from Canadian fishing vessels in
    the waters of the Pacific Ocean that are not Canadian fisheries
    waters; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the harvesting of marine plants from Canadian
    fisheries waters in the Pacific Ocean that are not within the
    geographical limits of the Province.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} These Regulations do not apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} sport fishing;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} fishing from a foreign fishing vessel, as defined
    in section 2 of the [Coastal Fisheries Protection
    Act](/eng/acts/C-33);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} aquaculture or prescribed activities, as defined in
    the [Pacific Aquaculture
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2010-270), in

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the territorial sea of Canada off the coast of
      British Columbia,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the internal waters of Canada off the coast of
      British Columbia that are not in that province,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the internal waters of Canada in British
      Columbia, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} any facility in British Columbia from which fish
      may escape into Canadian fisheries waters; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} fishing for marine mammals.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Sections 6, 8 to 10, 13 to 15, 22, 24 and 25, and
  Parts IV to VII and IX do not apply with respect to fishing and
  related activities carried out under the authority of a licence issued
  under the [Aboriginal Communal Fishing Licences
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-332).

- [(4) and (5)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2003-398, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-247, s. 1
- SOR/2002-225, s. 9
- SOR/2003-398, s. 1
- SOR/2010-270, s. 12
:::

## [Joined Nets]{.HTitleText1} {#h-956256 .Part}

**[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of these
Regulations, where two or more nets are joined together, they shall be
considered to be one net.

## [PART I]{.HLabel1}[General Provisions]{.HTitleText1} {#h-956258 .Part}

### [Import of Fish]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956259 .Subheading}

**[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** No person shall bring into
the Province any live fish of a species set out in Schedule VIII.

### [Prohibited Fishing Methods]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956261 .Subheading}

**[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** No person shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} fish by snagging or with snares.

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/99-296, s. 2\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-334, s. 2
- SOR/99-296, s. 2
- [SOR/2022-196, s. 14(F)](#1375854-1376846){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1375854-1376846.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-6-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 6"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Prohibition Against Molesting or Injuring Fish]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956267 .Subheading}

**[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** Subject to these
Regulations, no person shall molest or injure fish.

### [Lights Prohibited]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956269 .Subheading}

- **[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall use torches or artificial lights to attract or repel fish
  unless they are fishing for shrimp with a trawl net or for squid.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall use fishing gear that has a flashing
  light attached to it.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-132, s. 1](#1129341-1130841){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1129341-1130841.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-8-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 8"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Gill Net Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956273 .Subheading}

**[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** No person shall, in tidal
waters,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} fish with more than one gill net at one time; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} except where fishing for herring, fish with a gill
  net that has any part of the corkline under the surface of the water.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/94-391, s. 2
:::

### [Dip Net Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956279 .Subheading}

**[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish with
a dip net

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the area of the mouth of which exceeds 1 m^2^; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the depth of which is greater than 1.5 m.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-334, s. 3
:::

- **[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** [(1) and
  (2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/94-731, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/99-296, s. 4\]]{.Repealed}

**[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/99-296,
s. 5\]]{.Repealed}

### [Marking of Gear]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956289 .Subheading}

- **[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except
  as otherwise provided as a condition of a licence issued under these
  Regulations, no person shall fish with a gill net unless it is marked
  in accordance with this section.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A buoy floating on the surface of the water shall be
  attached to each end of every gill net that is not attached to a
  vessel.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where a gill net is used in commercial fishing for
  salmon,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the buoys referred to in subsection (2) shall be
    orange in colour and at least 125 cm in circumference; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the end of the gill net that is not attached to a
    vessel shall be marked with a lantern that gives a steady white
    light during the period beginning one hour after sunset and ending
    one hour before sunrise.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where a gill net is used in commercial fishing for
  roe herring,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the buoys referred to in subsection (2) shall be at
    least 125 cm in circumference; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} all buoys that are attached to the gill net shall
    be of the same colour.

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2013-37, s. 6\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote

**[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** Except as otherwise
provided as a condition of a licence issued under these Regulations, no
person shall fish with a longline unless it has attached to each end a
buoy floating on the surface of the water.

- **[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2) and except
  as otherwise provided as a condition of a licence issued under these
  Regulations, no person shall fish for commercial purposes with traps
  or ring nets unless a buoy floating on the surface of the water is
  attached to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} each end of every line of traps or ring nets; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} each trap or ring net that is not part of a line of
    traps or ring nets.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a person who is
  fishing for shellfish in Subarea 29-3, 29-4, 29-6, 29-7, 29-9 or 29-10
  during any period in which fishing for salmon with a net is authorized
  in that Subarea.

**[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may specify
a licence condition referred to in subsection 13(1), section 14 or
subsection 15(1) only if the purpose of the condition is to mitigate
marine mammal entanglement.

### [Off-Loading and Reporting of Salmon and Roe Herring Catches]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956309 .Subheading}

- **[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) and
  18(5), where salmon or roe herring has been caught by commercial
  fishing, no person shall off-load that salmon or roe herring at any
  place other than

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a licensed facility;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a registered vessel; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a vehicle whose operator holds a licence that is
    issued under the British Columbia Fish and Seafood Act, SBC 2015, c.
    14, as amended from time to time, and that authorizes the holder to
    receive commercially caught fish.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a person who catches
  salmon or roe herring and sells it directly to an individual in Canada
  for the purpose of consumption by that individual.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} An operator of a vehicle referred to in paragraph
  (1)(c) shall not off-load any salmon or roe herring received from a
  registered vessel at any place other than a licensed facility.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The operator of a licensed facility at which salmon
  or roe herring is off-loaded shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} provide information respecting each off-loading by
    completing the form supplied by the Minister for that purpose;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} mail a copy of the completed form within seven days
    after the date of the off-loading to the Statistics Unit of the
    Department of Fisheries and Oceans at 200-401 Burrard Street,
    Vancouver, British Columbia, V6C 3S4;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} keep a copy of the completed form for two years
    after the date of the off-loading; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} provide a copy of the completed form to a
    representative of the Department when requested to do so.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-58, s. 27
- [SOR/2022-196, s. 15](#1375856-1376853){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1375856-1376853.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-17-20170413.html "Link to previous version of section 17"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of this section,

  [[export zone]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[export zone]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any of the following
      zones:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Central Zone, which comprises Areas 6 to
        10, 106 to 110 and 130,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the North Zone, which comprises Areas 1 to 5,
        101 to 105 and 142, and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the South Zone, which comprises Areas 11 to 29,
        111 and 121 to 127; ([zone d'exportation]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[monthly allowable catch]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[monthly allowable catch]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the total
      weight of salmon from an export zone that may be caught and
      retained in a calendar month; ([prises mensuelles
      admissibles]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[total allowable catch]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[total allowable catch]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the total
      weight of salmon or roe herring, as the case may be, from an
      export zone that may be caught and retained in a calendar year.
      ([total des prises admissibles]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Regional Director-General may determine the
  monthly allowable catch for an export zone and shall give notice of
  the monthly allowable catch, prior to the commencement of any salmon
  fishery in any calendar month, to the owner or operator of each vessel
  in respect of which a licence has been issued under subsection (4).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Regional Director-General may determine the total
  allowable catch for an export zone and shall give notice of the total
  allowable catch, prior to the commencement of any salmon or roe
  herring fishery in any calendar year, to the owner or operator of each
  vessel in respect of which a licence has been issued under subsection
  (4).

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may issue, in respect of a vessel, a
  licence to off-load salmon or roe herring in the United States where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a Category D licence has been issued in respect of
    that vessel;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the vessel is suitably equipped to permit the
    weighing, counting and biological sampling of the salmon or roe
    herring that is taken on board the vessel; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the vessel has suitable accommodations for carrying
    representatives of the Department.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} The operator of a vessel in respect of which a
  licence has been issued under subsection (4) may off-load salmon or
  roe herring from the vessel in the United States if the salmon or roe
  herring was taken on board the vessel while a representative of the
  Department was on board.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may specify in a licence issued under
  subsection (4) conditions respecting

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the export zone or zones in which salmon or roe
    herring may be taken on board for the purpose of off-loading in the
    United States; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the port or ports in each export zone at which
    representatives of the Department shall be embarked and disembarked.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} No operator of a vessel in respect of which a licence
  has been issued under subsection (4) shall fail to comply with any
  condition set out in the licence.

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} No operator of a vessel in respect of which a licence
  has been issued under subsection (4) shall, during any calendar month,
  take on board salmon in an export zone for the purpose of off-loading
  in the United States after notification has been given to the operator
  by a representative of the Department that the total weight of salmon
  from that export zone taken on board vessels in that month for
  off-loading in the United States has reached 25 per cent of the
  monthly allowable catch for that export zone in the preceding calendar
  month, minus the weight of salmon exported from that export zone in
  the preceding calendar month under the authority of all licences
  issued under subsection (4), plus the greater of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 25 per cent of the monthly allowable catch for that
    export zone in the calender month in respect of which the
    calculation is being made, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the percentage, calculated for that calendar year
    pursuant to subsection (10), of the monthly allowable catch for that
    export zone in the calendar month in respect of which the
    calculation is being made.

- [(9)]{.lawlabel} No operator of a vessel in respect of which a licence
  has been issued under subsection (4) shall, during any calendar year,
  take on board roe herring in an export zone for the purpose of
  off-loading in the United States after notification has been given to
  the operator by a representative of the Department that the total
  weight of roe herring from that export zone taken on board vessels for
  off-loading in the United States has reached the greater of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 25 per cent of the total allowable catch for that
    export zone in that calendar year, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the percentage, calculated for that calendar year
    pursuant to subsection (10), of the total allowable catch for that
    export zone in that year.

- [(10)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of subsections (8) and (9), the
  percentage for a calendar year is calculated using the following
  formula:

  ::: {#956350 .Subsection}
  (100X ) ÷ Y

  where

  [X]{.dfn}
  :   is the average annual total weight of salmon or roe herring, as
      the case may be, that was caught in United States fishery waters
      and exported by sea from the United States to Canada without
      having being processed or landed in the United States during the
      four calendar years immediately preceding that year; and

  [Y]{.dfn}
  :   is the aggregate of the total allowable catches of salmon or roe
      herring, as the case may be, for all export zones in that year.
  :::

- [(11)]{.lawlabel} A representative of the Department who is on board a
  vessel in respect of which a licence has been issued under
  subsection (4) while salmon or roe herring is being taken on board for
  export to the United States shall, prior to disembarking the vessel,
  seal all of the vessel's fish holds.

- [(12)]{.lawlabel} No person other than a fishery officer shall tamper
  with or remove a seal that has been placed on a fish hold pursuant to
  subsection (11).

- [(13)]{.lawlabel} A licence issued under subsection (4) shall expire
  on December 31 of the year for which it was issued.

## [PART II]{.HLabel1}[Registration and Licensing of Fishers and Vessels]{.HTitleText1} {#h-956357 .Part}

### [Issuance of Registration Cards, Registration Certificates and Licences]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956358 .Subheading}

- **[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may issue a fisher's registration card, a vessel registration
  certificate or a licence referred to in column I of an item of Part I
  of Schedule II upon application therefor and payment of the fee set
  out in column II of that item.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where, pursuant to subsection (1), the Minister
  issues a fisher's registration card to a person or a vessel
  registration certificate in respect of a vessel, that person or vessel
  is deemed to be registered with the Department.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} If an Indian or the Northern Native Fishing
  Corporation is issued a Category A licence in respect of a vessel, the
  Indian or the Corporation must elect to pay either the applicable fee
  set out in subitem 3(1) of Part I of Schedule II or the applicable fee
  set out in subitem 3(2) of that Part.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where a vessel in respect of which a Category A
  licence was issued after December 31, 1979 for the fee set out in
  subitem 3(2) of Part I of Schedule II is sold to a person other than
  an Indian or the Northern Native Fishing Corporation, the Category A
  licence is void.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-196, s. 16](#1375867-1376856){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1375867-1376856.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-19-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 19"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Registration Certificate Invalid]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956364 .Subheading}

- **[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  vessel registration certificate issued in respect of a vessel becomes
  invalid if the vessel is modified so that the tonnage or length of the
  vessel differs from that set out in the vessel registration
  certificate.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a vessel registration certificate becomes
  invalid pursuant to subsection (1),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the vessel registration certificate and all
    licences issued in respect of the vessel shall be returned to the
    Minister within seven days after the day on which the certificate
    becomes invalid; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} no person shall use the vessel in commercial
    fishing until a new vessel registration certificate has been issued
    in respect of it.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-196, s. 17](#1375869-1376859){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1375869-1376859.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-20-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 20"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/96-330,
s. 2\]]{.Repealed}

### [Registration and Licence Required]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956371 .Subheading}

- **[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall use a vessel, and no owner or lessee of a vessel shall
  permit the use of that vessel, in commercial fishing for any species
  of fish unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (2), the vessel is
    registered; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the use of the vessel to fish for that species of
    fish is authorized by a commercial fishing licence.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person may use an unregistered vessel in commercial
  fishing for roe herring with a gill net or when fishing in non-tidal
  waters.

<!-- -->

- **[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), no person
  shall use a vessel to process fish unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the vessel is registered; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a Category P licence has been issued in respect of
    the vessel.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of washing,
  eviscerating, icing or freezing fish, other than bivalve molluscs, on
  board the vessel that was used to catch the fish.

**[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24 .sectionLabel}** No person shall use a
vessel to transport from fishing grounds fish that were taken in
commercial fishing unless the vessel is registered and

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} is licensed to be used in commercial fishing; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a Category D licence has been issued in respect of
  the vessel.

**[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25 .sectionLabel}** A person who is 16 years
of age or older shall not engage in commercial fishing or be on board a
vessel that is being used in commercial fishing unless they hold a
fisher's registration card.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-196, s. 18(E)](#1375871-1376862){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1375871-1376862.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-25-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 25"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), no person
  shall fish except under the authority of a licence issued under these
  Regulations, the [Fishery (General)
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-53) or the [Aboriginal Communal
  Fishing Licences Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-332).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a person who is
  registered and who is engaged in commercial fishing for a species of
  fish from a registered vessel that is authorized by a commercial
  fishing licence to be used in fishing for that species.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-334, s. 4
:::

## [PART III]{.HLabel1}[[\[Repealed, SOR/93-334, s. 5\]]{.Repealed}]{.HTitleText1} {#h-956391 .Part}

## [PART IV]{.HLabel1}[Fish Other than Halibut, Herring, Salmon and Shellfish]{.HTitleText1} {#h-956392 .Part}

**[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2003-137, s. 20\]]{.Repealed}

### [Close Times]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956394 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/94-57, s. 1

\]
:::

**[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30 .sectionLabel}** No person shall catch and
retain a species of fish set out in column I of an item of Schedule III
from the waters set out in column II of that item with the type of
fishing gear set out in column III of that item during the close time
set out in column IV of that item.

**[[31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/94-57,
s. 2\]]{.Repealed}

**[[32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/99-296,
s. 6\]]{.Repealed}

### [Lingcod]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956400 .Subheading}

**[[33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33 .sectionLabel}** No person shall catch and
retain a lingcod that is less than

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} 58 cm in length, measured from the tip of the nose to
  the tip of the tail; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} where the head has been removed, 45 cm in length,
  measured along the shortest length of the body to the tip of the tail.

### [Sablefish]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956404 .Subheading}

- **[[34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-34 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall catch and retain a sablefish that is less than

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 55 cm in length, measured from the tip of the nose
    to the fork of the tail; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where the head has been removed, 39 cm in length,
    measured from the origin of the first dorsal fin to the fork of the
    tail.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/99-296, s. 7\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/99-296, s. 7
:::

### [Smelt]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956412 .Subheading}

- **[[35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-35 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall fish for smelt with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a gill net that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is more than 275 m in length,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} has a mesh size of less than 25 mm, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} has a mesh size of more than 50 mm; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a seine that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is more than 90 m in length, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} has a mesh size of less than 19 mm.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall fish for smelt with an anchored gill
  net in any Area other than Areas 28 and 29.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person shall fish for smelt in Area 28 or 29
  during the period beginning at 08:00 h on Thursday and ending at
  08:00 h on the following Monday.

**[[36 and 37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-36_and_37
.sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/99-296, s. 8\]]{.Repealed}

## [PART V]{.HLabel1}[Herring]{.HTitleText1} {#h-956425 .Part}

**[[38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-38 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2003-137, s. 21\]]{.Repealed}

### [Close Times]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956427 .Subheading}

**[[39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-39 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
or catch and retain herring under the authority of a licence set out in
column I of an item of Schedule V from the waters set out in column II
of that item with the type of fishing gear set out in column III of that
item during the close time set out in column IV of that item.

**[[40]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-40 .sectionLabel}** Where notified by a
fishery officer or fishery guardian that a roe herring fishery is about
to be opened in an area, a person who is operating a fishing vessel in
that area shall, unless the vessel is authorized to be used in the roe
herring fishery in that area pursuant to a Category H licence, remove
the vessel from the area until the fishery has opened.

### [Herring Spawning Grounds]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956430 .Subheading}

- **[[41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  fishery officer may designate an area as a herring spawning ground and
  establish the boundaries of the area and the period of time for which
  the area is designated as a herring spawning ground.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where an area is designated as a herring spawning
  ground under subsection (1), notice of the designation shall be given
  by the fishery officer to the persons affected or likely to be
  affected by the designation by one or more of the methods set out in
  respect of the variation of close times in section 7 of the [Fishery
  (General) Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-53).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person shall, in an area designated as a herring
  spawning ground under subsection (1),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} transfer herring from one vessel to another; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} anchor any fishing vessel or any other vessel used
    to assist in herring fishing.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-196, s. 19(F)](#1375873-1376864){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1375873-1376864.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-41-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 41"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[42]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/99-296,
s. 9\]]{.Repealed}

**[[43]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-43 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/99-296,
s. 10\]]{.Repealed}

### [Herring Enclosures]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956439 .Subheading}

- **[[44]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-44 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall establish or operate a herring enclosure unless
  authorized to do so under a Category J or Z licence.

- [(2) and (3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/99-296, s. 11\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-334, s. 6
- SOR/99-296, s. 11
:::

### [Release of Herring from Herring Enclosure]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956445 .Subheading}

- **[[45]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-45 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  a fishery officer has evidence of excessive mortality of herring in a
  herring enclosure, the fishery officer may direct the operator of the
  enclosure to release all herring contained therein and, where the
  operator of the enclosure cannot be readily located, may release the
  herring from the enclosure.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall fail to comply with a direction given
  under subsection (1).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where a fishery officer releases herring from a
  herring enclosure under subsection (1), the fishery officer shall, as
  soon as practicable, notify the operator of the enclosure of the
  release.

### [Herring Spawn on Kelp]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956450 .Subheading}

**[[46]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/99-296,
s. 12\]]{.Repealed}

**[[47]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47 .sectionLabel}** No person shall transport
herring spawn on kelp in any container that is not marked with the
number of the licence under the authority of which the herring spawn on
kelp was taken or collected.

**[[48]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-48 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/99-296,
s. 13\]]{.Repealed}

## [PART VI]{.HLabel1}[Salmon]{.HTitleText1} {#h-956454 .Part}

**[[49]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2003-137, s. 22\]]{.Repealed}

### [General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956456 .Subheading}

**[[50]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-50 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/99-296,
s. 14\]]{.Repealed}

**[[51]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51 .sectionLabel}** No person shall drive or
attempt to drive salmon from one area of water to another.

### [Salmon Fishing Boundaries]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956459 .Subheading}

- **[[52]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  fishery officer may designate a salmon fishing boundary by erecting or
  causing to be erected

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of tidal waters, not more than one
    nautical mile from the mouth of a river, creek or stream, two
    orange- or yellow-coloured triangular signs, each side of which is
    at least 1.5 m in length and 12 cm in width; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of non-tidal waters, orange-coloured
    triangular signs, each side of which is at least 50 cm in length.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall fish for salmon

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in tidal waters within a circle, the diameter of
    which is the straight line between two orange-coloured signs erected
    under paragraph (1)(a);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in tidal waters between the mouth of a river, creek
    or stream and the straight line between two yellow-coloured signs
    erected under paragraph (1)(a); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in any non-tidal waters other than waters bounded
    by orange-coloured signs erected under paragraph (1)(b).

### [Salmon Close Times]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956468 .Subheading}

- **[[53]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall fish in any waters set out in column I of an item of Part
  I of Schedule VI for the species of salmon set out in column II of
  that item with the type of fishing gear set out in column III of that
  item during the close time set out in column IV of that item.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall troll in any waters set out in column
  I of an item of Part II of Schedule VI for the species of salmon set
  out in column II of that item using the type of vessel set out in
  column III of that item during the close time set out in column IV of
  that item.

<!-- -->

- **[[54]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-54 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  a close time set out in Part I of Schedule VI is varied pursuant to
  section 6 of the [Fishery (General)
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-53) to permit salmon fishing by
  gill net, the notice referred to in section 7 of the [Fishery
  (General) Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-53) shall specify one
  of the gill net mesh sizes set out in column I, one of the maximum
  gill net depth limits set out in column II, one of the maximum gill
  net hang ratios set out in column III, one of the minimum corkline to
  web distances set out in column V and one of the maximum corkline to
  web distances set out in column VI of the table to this section.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a close time has been varied to permit salmon
  fishing with a gill net in accordance with subsection (1), no person
  shall fish for salmon with a gill net that has

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any meshes that are not contiguous and uniform in
    size;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a mesh size other than that specified in the
    notice;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a depth greater than the maximum depth specified in
    the notice;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} webbing with a total length, when compared to the
    length of the corkline to which it is hung, in a ratio greater than
    the maximum hang ratio specified in the notice; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a corkline to web distance less than the minimum
    corkline to web distance or greater than the maximum corkline to web
    distance specified in the notice.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where a close time set out in an item of Part I of
  Schedule VI is varied pursuant to section 6 of the [Fishery (General)
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-53) to permit salmon fishing with
  a purse seine, the notice referred to in section 7 of the [Fishery
  (General) Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-53) shall specify one
  of the minimum purse seine bunt mesh sizes set out in column IV of the
  table to this section.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where a close time has been varied to permit salmon
  fishing with a purse seine in accordance with subsection (3), no
  person shall fish for salmon with a purse seine that has, in the final
  18 m of the bunt, a mesh size that is less than the minimum bunt mesh
  size specified in the notice.

### [Salmon Size Limits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956490 .Subheading}

- **[[55]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-55 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person who is trolling for salmon shall catch and retain a coho salmon
  that is less than

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 30 cm in length, measured from the tip of the nose
    to the fork of the tail; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where the head has been removed, 26 cm in length,
    measured along the shortest length of the body to the fork of the
    tail.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person who is trolling for salmon shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in any area seaward of the surfline or in any of
    Areas 1 to 12 or 19 to 27, catch and retain a chinook salmon that is
    less than

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} 67 cm in length, measured from the tip of the
      nose to the fork of the tail, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} where the head has been removed, 56 cm in
      length, measured along the shortest length of the body to the fork
      of the tail; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any of Areas 13 to 18, 28 or 29, catch and
    retain a chinook salmon that is less than

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} 62 cm in length, measured from the tip of the
      nose to the fork of the tail, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} where the head has been removed, 51 cm in
      length, measured along the shortest length of the body to the fork
      of the tail.

### [Salmon Trolling]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956502 .Subheading}

- **[[56]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-56
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/99-296, s.
  15\]]{.Repealed}

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/96-330, s. 3\]]{.Repealed}

- [(3) and (4)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/99-296, s. 15\]]{.Repealed}

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} No person shall, while trolling in an area in which
  trolling for a species of salmon is prohibited, have on board a vessel
  any salmon of that species.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/96-330, s. 3
- SOR/99-296, s. 15
:::

### [Salmon Gill Nets]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956510 .Subheading}

- **[[57]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-57 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall fish for salmon in the waters set out in column I of the
  table to this section during the close time set out in column II from
  a vessel where the aggregate length of gill net on the drum or drums
  of the vessel and in the water is greater than the net length set out
  in column III.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person who is fishing for salmon shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} set a gill net in the water and leave it
    unattended; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} use an anchored gill net in any waters except those
    of Subarea 10-11 or the Taku or Stikine Rivers.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/97-365, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/94-391, s. 4\]]{.Repealed}

### [Salmon Purse Seines]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956526 .Subheading}

**[[58]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/99-296,
s. 16\]]{.Repealed}

**[[59]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-59 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/99-296,
s. 17\]]{.Repealed}

- **[[60]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-60 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of this section,

  [[depth]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[depth]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a purse seine, means
      the sum total of the mesh sizes of all meshes in a perpendicular
      row of meshes from the corkline to the lead line;
      ([chute]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[length]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[length]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a purse seine, means
      the aggregate total length of the seine corkline and any lead
      attached to it and includes any part of the seine corkline on the
      net drum of the vessel from which it is being used.
      ([longueur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall fish for salmon with a purse seine
  that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has a mesh size of less than 70 mm;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is less than 270 m in length; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is less than 20 m in depth.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person shall fish for salmon in any Area other
  than Area 20 with a purse seine that is more than

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 400 m in length; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 52 m in depth.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} No person shall fish for salmon in Area 20 with a
  purse seine that is more than

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 550 m in length; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 80 m in depth.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-196, s. 22(F)](#1375887-1376873){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1375887-1376873.html"
  role="button"}
:::

## [PART VII]{.HLabel1}[Shellfish]{.HTitleText1} {#h-956543 .Part}

**[[61]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-61 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2003-137, s. 23\]]{.Repealed}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956545 .Subheading}

**[[62]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-62 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of this
Part, [[width]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a shellfish, means
the maximum distance, measured in a straight line, through the greatest
breadth of the shell of the shellfish.

### [Close Times]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956547 .Subheading}

**[[63]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-63 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish for
a species of shellfish set out in column I of an item of Schedule VII in
the waters set out in column II of that item using a method set out in
column III of that item during the close time set out in column IV of
that item.

**[[64]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/99-296,
s. 18\]]{.Repealed}

### [Clams]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956550 .Subheading}

- **[[65]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-65 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall take

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a razor clam that is less than 90 mm in width;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a butter clam that is less than 63 mm in width; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a Manila or littleneck clam that is less than 38 mm
    in width.

- [(2) and (3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/99-296, s. 19\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/99-296, s. 19
:::

### [Crabs]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956559 .Subheading}

- **[[66]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall catch and retain

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a Dungeness crab that is less than 165 mm in width;
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a red rock crab that is less than 115 mm in width.

- [(2) and (3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/99-296, s. 20\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/99-296, s. 20
:::

**[[67 to 69]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-67_to_69 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/99-296, s. 21\]]{.Repealed}

### [Sea Urchins]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956568 .Subheading}

- **[[70]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-70 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall catch and retain a red sea urchin that is less than
  100 mm in width.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall catch and retain a green sea urchin
  that is less than 55 mm in width.

## [PART VIII]{.HLabel1}[Marine Plants]{.HTitleText1} {#h-956572 .Part}

- **[[71]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-71 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall harvest marine plants except under the authority of a
  licence issued for that purpose by the Minister under section 45 of
  the Act.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person to whom a licence is issued under
  section 45 of the Act shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} maintain an accurate record showing the area where
    harvesting is carried out and the quantity of each species of marine
    plants harvested in that area; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} not later than the tenth day of every month during
    the term of the licence, and following the termination of the
    licence, make a written return to the Minister showing the quantity
    of each species of marine plants harvested under the authority of
    the licence during the previous month.

## [PART IX]{.HLabel1}[Halibut]{.HTitleText1} {#h-956578 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956579 .Subheading}

**[[72]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of this
Part,

[[automated hook stripper]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[automated hook stripper]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, commonly called a
    crucifier, means a device through which the groundline of a longline
    can be passed during gear retrieval, allowing the groundline and
    hooks to pass freely while removing the fish; ([dégorgeoir
    automatique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Commission]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Commission]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the International Pacific
    Halibut Commission established under the Convention between Canada
    and the United States of America for the Preservation of the Halibut
    Fishery of the Northern Pacific Ocean and Bering Sea, signed at
    Ottawa on March 2, 1953, and the Protocol amending that Convention,
    signed at Washington D.C. on March 29, 1979.
    ([Commission]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/94-728, s. 1
:::

**[[73]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-73 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2003-137, s. 24\]]{.Repealed}

### [Close Time]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956586 .Subheading}

- **[[74]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-74 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall fish for or catch and retain halibut in any Subarea
  during the close time from 12:00 h October 31 to 12:00 h March 1.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person fishing in any Subarea during the close
  time set out in subsection (1) shall possess halibut.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/94-728, s. 1
:::

### [Size Limits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956592 .Subheading}

**[[75]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75 .sectionLabel}** No person shall catch and
retain any halibut that is less than

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} 81.3 cm in length, measured in a straight line from
  the tip of the lower jaw with the mouth closed and passing over the
  pectoral fin to the extreme end of the middle of the tail; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} where the head has been removed, 61 cm in length,
  measured in a straight line from the base of the pectoral fin at its
  most anterior point to the extreme end of the middle of the tail.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/94-728, s. 1
:::

### [Gear Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956598 .Subheading}

- **[[76]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall fish for or catch and retain halibut with any gear other
  than hook and line or trap.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall possess halibut on board a vessel
  that is being used to fish with or is carrying a trawl net.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person shall fish for halibut from a vessel that
  is equipped with or has on board an automated hook stripper.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} No person shall possess halibut on a vessel that is
  equipped with or has on board an automated hook stripper.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/94-728, s. 1
- SOR/2008-280, s. 1
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-76-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 76"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Retention of Tagged Halibut]{.HTitleText2} {#h-956606 .Subheading}

**[[77]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77 .sectionLabel}** Notwithstanding anything
in these Regulations or the [Fishery (General)
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-53), where a person at any time
catches a halibut that bears a tag affixed by the Commission, the person
may retain the halibut, if at the time of the landing of the halibut the
person reports the landing to a fishery officer or a representative of
the Commission and makes the halibut, with the tag still affixed,
available for examination by a fishery officer or a representative of
the Commission.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/94-728, s. 1
:::
